After a command's writes and before its transaction commits, the\ncommand passes the accounts it touched to `balance.ensure_non_negative`. The function computes each\nnamed account's balance as the sum of its transaction amounts, in integer cents, from the database.\nIf any balance is below zero it raises `RejectedError` with `exit_code` set to `3`. The caller then\nrolls back the command's transaction, so no table changes.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `balance.ensure_non_negative(db, accounts)`: `accounts` is an iterable of account codes. Returns\n  `None` when every named balance is zero or positive. Raises `balance.RejectedError` otherwise.\n- `balance.RejectedError`: carries `exit_code`, which is `3`.\n- Reads: `db.accounts.list` to confirm each named account exists, and `db.transactions.list` to sum\n  amounts per account. Writes nothing.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Sum the `amount` integer-cent values of every transaction whose `account` equals each named code.\n2. An account with no transactions has balance `0`.\n3. A balance below `0` raises `RejectedError` for the first offending account in code order.\n4. Amounts are never converted to floating point at any step.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC nonneg-rejects-negative-balance ===\nIntent: A balance below zero raises RejectedError with exit code 3.\nimport tempfile\nimport os\n\nfrom persistence import open_database\nfrom balance import RejectedError, ensure_non_negative\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\ndb = open_database(os.path.join(work, date=\"2026-01-01\", account=\"CASH1\", category=\"OPENING\", amount=-500, memo=\"\")\n\nexpected_exit = 3\nraised = None\ntry:\n    ensure_non_negative(db, [\"CASH1\"])\nexcept RejectedError as error:\n    raised = error\nprint(\"raised:\", raised)\nassert raised is not None\nassert raised.exit_code == expected_exit\n=== END AC nonneg-rejects-negative-balance ===\n\n=== AC |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Provide `ledger init`, which drops and recreates the three ledger tables empty. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-DatabaseTarget.md, FEATURE-CommandTransaction.md, FEATURE-CliDispatch.md, FEATURE-CliOutput.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger init |\n| Consumes    | open_database, db.accounts.list, db.categories.list, db.transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger init` resets the store. It drops `accounts`, `categories`, and `transactions` if they exist,\nrecreates all three from the declared schema, and leaves them empty. It writes nothing to standard\noutput and exits `0`. It runs inside the command transaction, so a failure leaves the prior tables\nas they were.\n\nA missing SQLite file is created empty by the first command that opens it. If a command then finds\nno ledger tables, it exits `1` and writes nothing to standard output. The no-tables failure is\nexercised by the commands that read the tables; this feature verifies `init` itself.\n\n## Trigger\n\n`ledger init` with no further arguments. Usage errors (extra arguments) exit `2` under\nFEATURE-CliDispatch.md.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. Resolve the database target through FEATURE-DatabaseTarget.md.\n2. Open the store with `open_database`.\n3. Drop each declared table if present.\n4. Create each declared table empty, using the generated schema.\n5. Commit. Write nothing.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n- `ledger init`: exit `0` on success, `1` when the database is unreachable.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Writes: drops and recreates `accounts`, `categories`, and `transactions`.\n- Reads: none beyond the table existence check.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC init-leaves-tables-empty ===\nIntent: `ledger init` exits 0, writes nothing to stdout, and leaves all three tables empty.\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence import open_database\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = os.path.join(work, \"ledger.db\")\n\nexpected_exit = 0\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\nprint(\"stdout:\", repr(out), \"stderr:\", repr(err))\nassert code == expected_exit\nassert out == \"\"\n\ndb = open_database(os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"])\nassert len(db.accounts.list()) == 0\nassert len(db.categories.list()) == 0\nassert len(db.transactions.list()) == 0\n=== END AC init-leaves-tables-empty ===\n\n=== AC init-drops-existing-rows ===\nIntent: `ledger init` removes rows that existed before it ran.\nimport os\nimport Accounts\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Provide `ledger load accounts <csv>`, which loads every account row from a file or none. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CommandTransaction.md, FEATURE-ValidateText.md, FEATURE-CliDispatch.md, FEATURE-CliOutput.md, FEATURE-InitSchema.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger load accounts |\n| Consumes    | db.accounts.create, db.accounts.get, db.accounts.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger load accounts <csv>` reads an accounts file and inserts every row into the `accounts` table.\nThe whole load runs in one command transaction: if any row is rejected, no row is stored.\n\n## Trigger\n\n`ledger load accounts <path>`.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. Open the file. If it cannot be read, exit `1`.\n2. Check the header is exactly `account,name`. If it is not, exit `3`.\n3. Validate each row's code and name with the validators of FEATURE-ValidateText.md. An invalid row,\n   or a code repeated within the file or already present in the table, exits `3`.\n4. Insert every row with `db.accounts.create`, inside the command transaction.\n5. Write `table,loaded` then `accounts,<rows>` and exit `0`.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n- `ledger load accounts <csv>`: exit `0` on success; `1` unreadable file; `2` wrong argument count;\n  `3` rejected header or row, with nothing stored.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Writes: inserts the file's rows into `accounts`, all or none.\n- Reads: `accounts` for duplicate detection.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-accounts-seed-file ===\nIntent: Loading the staged seed accounts file exits 0 and stores exactly its data rows.\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nimport pathlib\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence import open_database\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = os.path.join(work, \"ledger.db\")\ncli_runner(\"init\")\n\nseed = pathlib.Path(\"sources/seed_accounts.csv\")\nexpected_rows = len(seed.read_text().splitlines()) - 1\n\nexpected_exit = 0\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", \"accounts\", str(seed))\nprint(\"stdout:\", repr(out), \"stderr:\", repr(err))\nassert code == expected_exit\nlines = out.splitlines()\nassert lines[0] == \"table,loaded\"\nassert lines[1].split(\",\") == [\"accounts\", str(expected_rows)]\n\ndb = open_database(os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"])\nassert len(db.accounts.list()) == expected_rows\n=== END AC load-accounts-seed-file ===\n\n=== AC BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-NonNegativeBalance.md ===\n# FEATURE: Non-Negative Balance\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Reject any command db.accounts.list, db.transactions.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGuards every mutating command. After a command's writes and before its transaction commits, the\ncommand passes the accounts it touched to `balance.ensure_non_negative`. The function computes each\nnamed account's balance as the sum of its transaction amounts, in integer cents, from the database.\nIf any balance is below zero it raises `RejectedError` with `exit_code` set to `3`. The caller then\nrolls back the command's transaction, so no table changes.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `balance.ensure_non_negative(db, accounts)`: `accounts` is an iterable of account codes. Returns\n  `None` when every named balance is zero or positive. Raises `balance.RejectedError` otherwise.\n- `balance.RejectedError`: carries `exit_code`, which is `3`.\n- Reads: `db.accounts.list` to confirm each named account exists, and `db.transactions.list` to sum\n  amounts per account. Writes nothing.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Sum the `amount` integer-cent values of every transaction whose `account` equals each named code.\n2. An account with no transactions has balance `0`.\n3. A balance below `0` raises `RejectedError` for the first offending account in code order.\n4. Amounts are never converted to floating point at any step.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC nonneg-rejects-negative-balance ===\nIntent: A balance below zero raises RejectedError with exit code Accounts\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Provide `ledger load accounts <csv>`, which loads every account row from a file or none. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CommandTransaction.md, FEATURE-ValidateText.md, FEATURE-CliDispatch.md, FEATURE-CliOutput.md, FEATURE-InitSchema.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger load accounts |\n| Consumes    | db.accounts.create, db.accounts.get, db.accounts.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger load accounts <csv>` reads an accounts file and inserts every row into the `accounts` table.\nThe whole load runs in one command transaction: if any row is rejected, no row is stored.\n\n## Trigger\n\n`ledger load accounts <path>`.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. Open the file. If it cannot be read, exit `1`.\n2. Check the header is exactly `account,name`. If it is not, exit `3`.\n3. Validate each row's code and name with the validators of FEATURE-ValidateText.md. An invalid row,\n   or a code repeated within the file or already present in the table, exits `3`.\n4. Insert every row with `db.accounts.create`, inside the command transaction.\n5. Write `table,loaded` then `accounts,<rows>` and exit `0`.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n- `ledger load accounts <csv>`: exit `0` on success; `1` unreadable file; `2` wrong argument count;\n  `3` rejected header or row, with nothing stored.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Writes: inserts the file's rows into `accounts`, all or none.\n- Reads: `accounts` for duplicate detection.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-accounts-seed-file ===\nIntent: Loading the staged seed accounts file exits 0 and stores exactly its data rows.\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nimport pathlib\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence import open_database\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = os.path.join(work, \"ledger.db\")\ncli_runner(\"init\")\n\nseed = FEATURE-LoadCategories.md ===\n# FEATURE: Load Categories\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Provide `ledger load categories <csv>`, which loads every category row from a file or none. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CommandTransaction.md, FEATURE-ValidateText.md, FEATURE-CliDispatch.md, FEATURE-CliOutput.md, FEATURE-InitSchema.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides    | ledger load categories |\n| Consumes    | db.categories.create, db.categories.get, db.categories.list |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger load categories <csv>` reads a categories file and inserts every row into the `categories`\ntable. The whole load runs in one command transaction: if any row is rejected, no row is stored.\n\n## Trigger\n\n`ledger load categories <path>`.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. Open the file. If it cannot be read, exit `1`.\n2. Check the header is exactly `category,description`. If it is not, exit `3`.\n3. Validate each row's code and description with the validators of FEATURE-ValidateText.md. An\n   invalid row, or a code repeated within the file or already present in the table, exits `3`.\n4. Insert every row with `db.categories.create`, inside the command transaction.\n5. Write `table,loaded` then `categories,<rows>` and exit `0`.\n\n## Routes and Interfaces\n\n- `ledger load categories <csv>`: exit `0` on success; `1` unreadable file; `2` wrong argument count;\n  `3` rejected header or row, with nothing stored.\n\n## Reads and Writes\n\n- Writes: inserts the file's rows into `categories`, all or none.\n- Reads: `categories` for duplicate detection.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-categories-seed-file ===\nIntent: Loading the staged seed categories file exits 0 and stores exactly its data rows.\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nimport pathlib\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence import open_database\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nos.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = os.path.join(work, \"ledger.db\")\ncli_runner(\"init\")\n\nseed = pathlib.Path(\"sources/seed_categories.csv\")\nexpected_rows = len(seed.read_text().splitlines()) - 1\n\nexpected_exit = 0\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", \"categories\", str(seed))\nprint(\"stdout:\", repr(out), \"stderr:\", repr(err))\nassert code == expected_exit\nlines = out.splitlines()\nassert lines[0] == \"table,loaded\"\nassert lines[1].split(\",\") == [\"categories\", str(expected_rows)]\n\ndb = open_database(os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"])\nassert len(db.categories.list()) == expected_rows\n=== END
